38cef5625 · Cristiano Calcagno · Nov 10, 14:43

Prototype: search and check a file's units in one process (--verify-units)

no-behaviour-change

Why

A unit's verdict takes two verifier processes: the search lowers the unit's slice, searches, checks its result, and writes the evidence; the check lowers the file, parses, translates and decodes that evidence, and checks it again. The command measures what one process over the file's one front costs.

addedCommand verifier --verify-units <mode> <ast> <claims> <file-key> <depth> [<names>]

the file's plans once (filePlans); each selected group searched over the file's own store and checked there; a verdict line per unit as --check-units prints it. mode 0: produceGroup, then unitVerdict at its encoded evidence mode 1: verifyGroup, the bundle checked as built mode 2: verifyGroup, the search leaving the graph's check to that check (produceUsing's unchecked) A measuring prototype: the pipeline does not call it, and the acceptance theorem does not state its modes 1 and 2.

85de2f539 · Cristiano Calcagno · Nov 10, 15:16

Prototype: a single forward pass over a unit's graph (--verify-units 3)

no-behaviour-change

Why

The search holds every fact at slots that each bind renumbers, so each node rewrites its whole knowledge. The pass names each binding once and keeps one store of facts over names, so a node touches only what its step reads.

addedDesign docs/drafts/single-pass.md

stable names; one store indexed by name; one visit per node in a topological order of the graph without the edges into cuts; a cut's state its given invariant (written assertion and the claims' facts), every edge into it an obligation; the proved step rules applied to the facts over the names a step reads.

addedCommand verifier --verify-units <mode> ..., modes 3, 4 and 5

mode 3: the pass (SinglePass.run), no certificate built; with TSCERT_COMPARE=1 each node's state is checked to entail the knowledge the existing search's certificate holds there. mode 4: the graph's shape statistics; mode 5: a node of the existing search's certificate (TSCERT_NODE), for debugging. expressionEffectUsing takes the types a node's derivation is sought among first (own?); by default its sequent's, as before.

Parts
lean-rules, docs
Lean
SinglePass.run · SinglePass.materialize · SinglePass.applyDelta · SinglePassStats.stats · SourceClaimJson.FilePlans.passGroup · CertificateProducer.expressionEffectUsing
Trust
none
Measured
measureAnalysis (pretext ba09794f), one run: mode 3 accepted, pass 1322 ms (mode 2 forward pass 4981 ms) · 5844 nodes visited once, 1885 rule applications (140 over the whole store), 4099 reads named as their slot, 347 entailment queries (mean 32 facts) · with TSCERT_COMPARE=1 every node's state entails the existing certificate's knowledge.

Decide a call's precondition once, where reached; the pass reads slot types by identity

no-behaviour-change

Why

A call's search decided every signature's precondition before trying any, and each again as it was tried; on measureAnalysis these entailment queries were most of the single pass's time. The pass also compared every slot's type structurally on each edge and asked of each edge's whole context whether no environment meets it.

modifiedSearch CertificateProducer.expressionWithUsing, a call's signatures

modifiedPass SinglePass.run

a step that may write selects its rule's output over every fact once (Checker.select), its derivation searched over the facts it names; a cut's given invariant kept at its slots and named at its visit; slot types compared by identity first (same); a node's unsatisfiability known from its first entering edge; mappings read by a cursor.

The single pass carries implications over names and drops names leaving scope; agreement script

no-behaviour-change

Why

The pass refused 326 units the pipeline verifies: a branch's types are refined by what a test or a promise call established earlier (implications), and an edge reads its source's links on every slot of a name. Its materializations also eliminated, at every read, the names no slot held any longer.

modifiedPass SinglePass.run, docs/drafts/single-pass.md

a state carries the implications its edges establish, over names; a step's yields (a test's, a promise call's, or a tested name's read) refine an edge whose types the plain comparison refuses, as produceNodeAt does; a name an edge leaves out of every slot is eliminated from the store there, once (Store.drop); a request's whole-certificate check the pass does not make (footprint, ensures, store regions) leaves the graph passed and the unit not handled.

addedScript dev/single-pass-agreement.mts

each pipeline case checked with its run kept, the pass run over the same fragment and claims, each unit's two verdicts compared; prints the disagreements with the pass's refusal and the counts by construct not handled.

877ddd953 · Cristiano Calcagno · Nov 10, 17:03

The single pass takes a given invariant at loop heads only; other statement entries are ordinary

no-behaviour-change

Why

The pass gave every statement entry its claims' facts as given: 215 entries and 5892 facts on measureAnalysis. By design only a loop head takes a given invariant; the pass carries its own state through every other statement entry.

modifiedPass SinglePass.run, docs/drafts/single-pass.md "4. Loop heads"

loop heads are the targets of retreating edges of a depth-first walk from the start; another statement entry is an ordinary node, its written assertion an obligation on its state; at a merge a linear bound every predecessor states of one form is kept at the weakest bound; an edge into a loop head that does not establish its given facts names them.

The single pass uses the proved call layers' output and keeps the forward facts loop heads need

no-behaviour-change

Why

modifiedPass SinglePass.run, docs/drafts/single-pass.md

modifiedSearch CertificateProducer.produceRawUsing

the guarded facts a merge keeps computed by joinGuarded, the same computation as before.

cc1a45da5 · Cristiano Calcagno · Nov 10, 17:11

The single pass reads what the selected units' groups read; one relabeling path

no-behaviour-change

Why

--verify-units 3 made its plans from the whole file: every qualified read of the importer and of each module it reads, planned recursively, and every function's claims lowered into the store and decoded. A selected unit needs only its group's claims, the other functions' signatures, and the reads its group's code names. SourceRequest.over kept a second relabeling path only to word an internal refusal as before.

modifiedCommand verifier --verify-units, mode 3

its plans are unitPlans'. A broken import or claim that the selected units' groups do not read no longer refuses those units, and a unit's dependencies name the modules its group reads, not the modules those read in turn. Every other mode, --produce-unit, --check-units and --check-module read filePlans as before.

addedPlans Claims/Json.lean, unitPlans

the groups the selection names are read off a front whose functions' claims are their signatures alone (signatureClaims: point claims and storage contracts left empty); the import plan holds the reads whose local names the groups' code mentions; the front holds the groups' functions' claims whole and every other function's signature claims (keepClaims); the groups are extracted over it as filePlans extracts them.

Import plan modified (Claims/Imports.lean, planImports)

with uses, it plans the importer's reads whose local name uses holds (readLocalName), and the modules they name, each restricted to the read closure of its units read, without planning that module's own reads; without uses it is unchanged.

modifiedRelabeling SourceRequests.lean, SourceRequest.over

the obligations, point claims and node plans are relabeled by partsOver alone; a refusal there is worded by the part that refuses.

lean-rules
Lean

no-behaviour-change

Why

addedCommand verifier --verify-units 3, TSCERT_OBSERVE

node:L or offset:K runs the pass again and stops at that node: its state on entry, after its step, its implications, and at a merge each predecessor's state at the merge's slots with the names each gives every slot; a node whose step is refused, on entry. refusal keeps each node's states and a merge's renamings, and at the refusal gives the obligation, the facts over its names and those connected, the facts it lacks, and each fact over those names an earlier state held, with where it was last present and what removed it (Trace.locate, Trace.lost).

modifiedSearch SinglePass.run

- a merge renames a name a predecessor holds at several slots to each fresh name those slots take (prepareRichInline's safeInteger(itemStart) was kept at one slot only); - an unconditional fact is stored with no condition polarity (two spellings of one bound compared unequal at merges); - a step's facts relate every slot holding one of their names by an alias link (24 property reads at a narrowed alias); - a call's precondition reads the facts reached through read links (5 calls at a path's length); - an edge observing a test releases the alternatives it contradicts (withElements; 4 calls under a disjunctive precondition); - a merge keeps a linear bound each predecessor entails (entailsKnown), a guard lowered through two reads, and a fact guarded by a tag another predecessor holds; implications join as the search joins them (meetImplications); a step's yields read its implications; - a delta's linear fact over a read value is also stated over the read's path (restateReads); - an edge its refined context fails is checked with each slot the target does not admit at its unrefined type.

5079d6cce · Cristiano Calcagno · Nov 10, 21:47

Compute a unit's expression and binding types in one forward walk, and compare them to the claims

no-behaviour-change

Why

The end state has no producer: TScert computes the types the lowering reads. This first stage computes them beside the claims and measures the two against each other, construct by construct, before the lowering reads either.

addedFunction lean/Verification/Source/Lowering/Synthesis.lean

one forward walk over a unit's fragment body in evaluation order. A literal is its singleton; a parameter or annotated declaration its written type; an identifier its binding's type narrowed by the tests on the path (truthiness, typeof and shape tests, strict equality with a one-value operand, discriminants, family guards); a member or element read the field or position the receiver's type states; a call the result of the signature its actuals select, a generic one instantiated by matching each binder against the receiver and the actuals (a binder, an array of it, a function returning it) or at explicit type arguments; an operator, comparison, conditional, ??, !, cast or literal by the language's typing of the construct. A let declares its initializer's type widened, an empty array literal takes the declared type it is assigned to, […] as const is a readonly tuple. A loop head holds each binding the loop assigns at its declared type; a merge the union of the arms. No search, fixed point or retry: what the rule does not determine is recorded as undetermined with its reason. Untrusted: nothing reads it but the comparison.

addedMode verifier --verify-units 7

for each selected unit, the computed type against the claimed one at every expression occurrence and statement entry the claims name: identical, wider, narrower, unrelated or undetermined, counted per construct kind, with each occurrence that is not identical.

the comparison over the pipeline cases, summed per construct kind.

e57b2655c · Cristiano Calcagno · Nov 11, 04:42

Make each value at the type its position expects

no-behaviour-change

Why

The claims-free front's largest group of disagreements: a store made at its own type where its position expects another. A store's contract is fixed where the store is made, and a read is a copy, an upcast of an immutable value or an upcast to a readonly view, so a literal, a function literal or a fresh call result made at its own type cannot be read at the expected one. The walk passed an expected type through five positions and fitted literals afterwards; an object literal at a dictionary, an array literal at a tuple or an iterable, an allocation of fields the expected record states as views, a const assertion's operand and a function literal inside a literal kept their own types.

modifiedFunction lean/Verification/Source/Lowering/Synthesis.lean

expr takes the type expected of its value and passes it to a conditional's arms, the right operand of &&, ||, ?? and ,, a cast's operand, an object literal's fields, an array literal's positions and a function literal; returns, annotated declarations, assignments (??=, ||=, &&= too), field and element writes and an expression body give it. fit makes an object literal at a dictionary with a writable index as that dictionary over its own keys, at a record with every key a field the allocation writes; an array literal at the one array member whose positions admit its elements, at an iterable at the readonly array of what it yields (iteratedElement); with nothing expected an array literal's presence is readable. A call's binders are read off the receiver and actuals first and the rest off the expected type, which wins where the two conflict; a fresh result is made at the expected array (freshAt). A […] as const or {…} as const operand is made at its const type.

modifiedDocument docs/drafts/single-pass.md

section 9 states the expected-type rule and the new agreement.

903f6b9f9 · Cristiano Calcagno · Nov 11, 06:07

Propose each pure declaration's footprint from its own walk

no-behaviour-change

Baseline edited

dev/perf-baseline.json is rewritten by perf-gate.mts --update to pass the gate. buildEngineProfile from the fragment rises from 122 to 172 ms CPU (+41%), measureAnalysis from 781 to 825 (+6%), prepareRichInline from 1904 to 1990 (+5%): each file's claims and front are built a second time where a declaration's footprint is found (fileFront 34 to 69-74 ms for buildEngineProfile, measured with TSCERT_PROFILE), and the declarations' walks take 10-16 ms. Counters do not rise (rules 248 to 247, whole-store rules 1 to 0 for buildEngineProfile). The other units' CPU rose 2-4% on a loaded machine.

Why

Units of the claims-free front were refused at a call of a file function read at the ordinary effect, whose footprint the producer had inferred: a callee changing nothing was read as changing anything.

addedFunction lean/Verification/Source/Claims/FragmentFront.lean

declarationEffects: each function a wanted unit reaches, callees first, walked as its unit is with each callee read at the footprint found for it; a walk changing nothing proposes keep/keep, bounding its throws where nothing leaves one unbounded; a self-recursive function is walked with itself at keep/keep bounding its throws, then without the bound where needed. Async, generic and rest functions, written effects, promises and stand-ins keep theirs. Each proposal is a stand-in its unit establishes; fragmentPlans builds the claims and the front again where any is found. TSCERT_FORCE=throws claims every function at keep/keep bounding its throws, the adversarial proposal.

modifiedFunction lean/Verification/Source/Lowering/Synthesis.lean

a unit's own parameters and declarations are its locals; for a declaration's footprint (Walk.strict), an assignment of a binding held in a cell (cellLocals) or an early binding's declaration, a call of an unknown callee, a call passing a function, an iteration, a call of a local binding at its declared type's effect, and a call without onThrow a handler catches each change the heap; an iteration, a finalizer and a call without onThrow leave a throw unbounded.

modifiedScript dev/single-pass-agreement.mts

a fourth control, invalid-exceptional-handler-fact wrong@336 under throws, expected refused.

modifiedDocument docs/drafts/single-pass.md

A subclass constructs its base through Base.[[class]] and copies the base instance's own fields into its own instance; the walk stated the class value with one construct signature, at the declared instance type, whose own keys are unknown, so the copy ({ ...Derived.[[base]] }) could not be stated at its exact keys and the derived constructor's code was refused (class-inheritance, class-static-inheritance). Error subclasses lower new Base(...)'s actuals and a later read of the base instance to expressions of one span in two statements, and the walk's one type per span gave the actual the read's type (error-subclass: parameter 0 claimed string). A class binding whose code is an earlier class binding's constructor (Base bound to Base.[[class]]'s lambda) walked that constructor again, as no fresh one, and replaced the allocation's type. An instance allocation was fitted at the declared instance type without its own-key bound, and its prototype link was read off a declared type that has none (nested-witness-optional-receiver).

Read a call's spread actuals as the call reads them

addedFunction walk: a call's spread actuals, spreadCall

A spread of a tuple of required positions stands for those positions. A spread of any other array stands for one actual of its elements; the callee is then read at its variadic signatures taking no more fixed parameters than the actuals before the first such spread (its elements are checked against the rest's element by the step). Into a callee of no variadic signature, no actual after the first spread may run code, since the call reads the spreads' elements after every actual (expression_calls.ml, the variadic and fixed-parameter branches).

1e569e83c · Cristiano Calcagno · Nov 11, 07:47

Copy each label of a spread's operand, as the producer's merge reads it

no-behaviour-change

Why

The walk's object spread and Object.assign took the operand's type as one record clause and stopped otherwise ("a spread of a value of no one record contract"): { ...(cond ? { y } : {}) }, { ...Point } of a class's statics, { ...x } of an intersection, of a number or of a string, Object.assign of such a source (diag S2, 63 units). It also kept an earlier field after a source that may hold that key at a type it does not state, a copy the check then refused.

addedFunction walk: a copy's alternatives, copiesOf, mergeCopy, copyType, copyParts

A copy is a union of alternatives, each its fields (optional or not) and whether its own keys are exactly theirs (object_spread.ml). A named field is set in each alternative; a spread merges each alternative with each copy its operand gives, one per label: nothing from null, undefined or a primitive other than a string, its keys exact; no stated key and no bound from a string or a function; a record's fields but its store, its prototype link (an Object.assign target's kept) and a key it also states as a method, its optional fields optional, a key two operands of an intersection state at the field within the other's, its keys exact where it bounds them (an array's never); a class value's static fields, no bound. The merge: a key the source surely has takes its type; one the copy has keeps its own where the source never has it, the union of both where it may; a key only the source may have is optional where the copy's keys are exact; a key either may hold at an unknown type is dropped (merge_alternative). At most 64 alternatives.

modifiedFunction walk: expr .build, .objectAssign

modifiedDocument docs/drafts/single-pass.md

Effect

  • generic-closure-effect positive new → verified a generic closure's write, the field tested again after the call
    // A generic closure that writes the heap is claimed at an effect covering
// the write: its caller tests the written field again after the call.
export function reread(box: { value: number | null }): number {
  const reset = <T>(x: T): T => {
    box.value = null;
    return x;
  };
  if (box.value !== null) {
    const kept = reset(box.value);
    return box.value === null ? kept : box.value;
  }
  return 0;
}
  • invalid-generic-closure-effect negative new → refused the field returned as narrowed after the closure's write
    // A generic closure's write ends its caller's narrowing of the field it
// writes: after the call the field holds `null`, which the function returns
// where it promises a number.
export function stale(box: { value: number | null }): number {
  const reset = <T>(x: T): T => {
    box.value = null;
    return x;
  };
  if (box.value !== null) {
    reset(0);
    return box.value;
  }
  return 0;
}

Control a function literal's default effect

verified
closure-default-effect.ts#rereadinvalid-closure-default-effect.ts#stale

Why

modifiedLowering the walk: a function literal's defaults under a forced proposal

walkLambda under TSCERT_FORCE=defaults leaves its defaults' changes
out of the literal's effect, the adversarial proposal.

modifiedScript dev/single-pass-agreement.mts

a control, invalid-closure-default-effect stale@264 under defaults, expected refused (refused at "code 0: footprint at node 1").

modifiedDocument docs/drafts/single-pass.md

section 9, "Function literals", the forced proposal; section 8, the control.

Effect

  • closure-default-effect positive new → verified a default's write, the field tested again after the call
    // A function literal of no written effect whose parameter default writes a
// field its caller narrowed: the default runs at a call omitting its
// argument, so the literal's effect is its default's write, and the caller
// tests the field again after the call.
export function reread(box: { value: number | null }): number {
  const read = (n: number, reset: null = (box.value = null)): number => n;
  if (box.value !== null) {
    const kept = read(box.value);
    return box.value === null ? kept : box.value;
  }
  return 0;
}
  • invalid-closure-default-effect negative new → refused the field returned as narrowed after a call running the default
    // A function literal of no written effect whose parameter default writes a
// field its caller narrowed: a call omitting the argument runs the default,
// after which the field holds `null`, which the caller returns where it
// promises a number.
export function stale(box: { value: number | null }): number {
  const read = (n: number, reset: null = (box.value = null)): number => n;
  if (box.value !== null) {
    read(0);
    return box.value;
  }
  return 0;
}

46e100471 · Cristiano Calcagno · Nov 11, 11:55

Bind a member at any receiver at its first signature of no promise, as a for-of steps it

refused
invalid-for-of-program-iterable-drain.ts#wrong

dev/perf-baseline.json: measureAnalysis from the fragment, rules 1330 -> 1329, at this branch's previous commit too: not this commit's.

Why

A for...of over a program's iterable binds its iterator's next (%next@… = %iterator@….next.bind(%iterator@…), the parser's protocolForOf). The walk stated the bound member at the member's own type, both rows of next(...[value]: [] | [TNext]) at the iterator's receiver, where the producer binds the first signature of no promise at any receiver (Bind, expression_values.ml:321-386), so the loop's step was refused (rediag-remaining ITER-NEXT, 41 units).

modifiedLowering the walk: a member bound to its object

expr's .bindWithReceiver of a member (o.m.bind(o)) states the
member at any receiver at the effect binding keeps (Effect.drop 0):
its first fixed signature of no promise binding keeps
(Effect.keeps 0), with each further one of its parameters
promising, else its one variadic signature; the member read itself
keeps the member's type. TSCERT_FORCE=binds states the bound member
keeping values and stores, the adversarial proposal.

addedCounterexample

an iterator whose `next` pops the array a length test guarded:after the loop `xs[0]` may be undefined (under Node `wrong([1])`returns undefined).

modifiedTooling dev/single-pass-agreement.mts: a control

a control, `invalid-for-of-program-iterable-drain` `wrong@533` under`binds`, expected refused (refused at "expression at node 12"; theloop's exit carries the given head facts alone, so it is refusedunforced too).

modifiedDocs docs/drafts/single-pass.md

section 9, "Function forms": the member bind; section 8, the control.

Effect

  • invalid-for-of-program-iterable-drain negative new -> refused the array popped by the iterator, read by index after the loop
    // The iterator's `next` pops the array a length test guarded before the
// loop: after the loop the array may be empty, so `xs[0]` may be undefined
// where a number is returned.
class Draining {
  constructor(readonly items: number[]) {}
  [Symbol.iterator](): Iterator<number> {
    const items = this.items;
    return {
      next: (): IteratorResult<number> => {
        const v = items.pop();
        return v === undefined ? { done: true, value: undefined } : { done: false, value: v };
      },
    };
  }
}
export function wrong(xs: number[]): number {
  if (xs.length < 1) return 0;
  let n = 0;
  for (const v of new Draining(xs)) n = n + v;
  return xs[0];
}

c7477caf8 · Cristiano Calcagno · Nov 11, 12:57

State an unreached expression the walk cannot type at the empty type

no-behaviour-change

Why

Measured first: measureAnalysis from the fragment states 22 queries and 624 query facts (baseline 17, 575), as at the three previous commits. no-return-callee-map rejected: after fail(), a call returning never, throw new Error("unreached") constructs Error, which the unreached state holds at the empty type, so the walk did not determine the construction and the unit was not handled.

modifiedTyping the walk's unreached code

Effect

  • 10 units -> agree: no-return-callee-map init, rejected, rejecting,
  • invalid-async-throw-only run, -method init, -static init (not handled
  • -> accepted, all pipeline-verified), switch-enum-case-narrowing
  • middleDefault (not handled -> accepted), invalid-async-throw-only-arrow,
  • -expression, -object-method wrong (not handled -> refused).

Soundness

The empty type is stated only where the walk's state is unreached (a statement after a call of no value, an arm a test leaves a binding no value): the node's context is then unsatisfiable, and an edge into it from a reached node is checked by the pass against it. The existing forced proposal never (every call's statement completing at no value) keeps invalid-closure-graph-captured-cell counter refused; the invalid-async-throw-only-* negatives are refused (were not handled).

Gap

P-UNREACHED (try-catch both, try-finally-jump switchThroughFinally, exitState, jumpFromCatch, finallyOverridesJump): a finalizer's throw copy or a handler no throw reaches shares its statement entries with the reached copies, and the lowering fixes a handler's %thrown@…% binding at unknown (Flow.lean:133), so its context is satisfiable whatever the walk states; the pass declines such a node (SinglePass.lean:1782-1787). Closing them needs per-copy statement entries in the walk and the claims' mapping, or the pass checking an unreached node at its own context (a change of its checking logic). closure-graph-entries genericDefault and for-of-iterable-parameter first sit behind other lanes' causes.

section 9's narrowing states the rule.

Compile the walk's mutual block within Lean's default limit

no-behaviour-change

Why

The walk's mutual block (expr, stmt, applyCall, walkLambda and their helpers) exceeded the code generator's default 200000 heartbeats, and the module raised the limit to 400000. Lean's code generator counts the heartbeats of a mutual block as one command: moving arms between functions of the block leaves the count unchanged, removing functions from the block lowers it.

modifiedFunction lean/Verification/Source/Lowering/Synthesis.lean

exprAt's call arms (a construction, a call, a call at a receiver, apply, bind and an invocation) form exprCall, which exprAt calls for them. record, recordAt, attempt, afterCall, decidedTest, fit, promised, collectBreaks, useHints and declarationHint call no function of the block and precede it, and useIn and useNested form a mutual block of their own. The module sets no heartbeat limit.

08a8734e4 · Cristiano Calcagno · Nov 11, 20:25

Specify canonical component interfaces and their replay boundary

no-behaviour-change

Why

Each importer repeats its imported modules' walks and checks. Publishing ordinary own-file judgments would admit measurement stand-ins and mismatched given identities; recursive cache entries leave cycles undefined.

modifiedDocs docs/drafts/module-interfaces.md

Certified imports supply the meaning of a footprint, stage replay its exact-input reuse. One canonical component computation excludes measurement stand-ins, normalizes given identities, checks complete required groups, and closes their dependencies before publication. Strongly connected components form a dependency DAG with canonical ordering. Written effects use the same successful-check rule as inferred effects. The request and process manifest bind every semantic input. Dependencies precede parent claims; failures publish no interface. Cold, warm and concurrent costs are separate measurements.
